### [74] [Fast and Accurate: An Adaptive VLA Inference Framework through Environment-aware Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06434)
*Yuewei Sun,Lang Qin,Zechuan Tian,Jingwen Li,Guiqin Wang,Shengzeng Huo,Wenxin Ren,Tao Fang,Xiaochen Zhang,Guanqing Deng,Xiang Wang,Xiaowen Dong,Qinghai Guo,Yuxin Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EMS（Environment-aware Model Selection），一种自适应VLA推理框架，通过强化学习策略在完全解耦的大型深思系统和轻量反应系统之间动态切换，在LIBERO基准上达到与大模型相当的成功率同时将有效动作频率提升至93.4Hz。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双系统VLA架构将快速模块与慢速模块的中间表征紧密耦合，需要端到端联合训练，限制了模块化、可扩展性和灵活的系統切换能力。

Method: 提出EMS框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)大型深思系统负责全局一致的轨迹规划；(2)轻量反应系统负责高频闭环控制；(3)基于强化学习的切换策略根据实时反馈动态选择调用哪个系统，实现慢系统的稀疏使用以平衡预训练知识利用与运行效率。

Result: 在LIBERO基准上，EMS达到与大规模基线相当的成功率，同时将有效动作频率提升至93.4Hz；在真实世界双臂操作任务中展现出强可扩展性，加速任务完成同时保持稳健性能。

Conclusion: 完全解耦的模块化双系统架构支持即插即用的模型替换，结合自适应环境感知切换策略和高频响应，为具身智能中长时程推理与实时反馈控制的平衡提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence demands both long-horizon reasoning and real-time closed-loop responsiveness. Recent dual-system Vision-Language-Action (VLA) architectures combine fast reactive control with slow deliberative reasoning to balance inference speed and task success rate. However, existing dual-process VLAs tightly couple the fast module to intermediate representations of the slow module, necessitating end-to-end joint training and limiting modularity, extensibility and flexible system switching. In this paper, we propose Environment-aware Model Selection (EMS), an adaptive VLA inference framework that switches between two fully decoupled systems of different scales through environment-aware model selection. The large-scale deliberative system provides globally consistent trajectory planning to ensure task success, while a lightweight reactive system enables high-frequency closed-loop control. A reinforcement-learning-based switching policy dynamically selects which system to invoke based on real-time feedback, enabling sparse use of the slow system and thereby balancing pretrained knowledge utilisation with runtime efficiency. Our design offers three key advantages over prior hierarchical VLA frameworks: (1) a fully decoupled and modular dual-system architecture that supports plug-and-play model replacement; (2) an adaptive, environment-aware switching strategy; (3) high-frequency inference for responsive closed-loop control. We extensively evaluate EMS in both simulation and real-world environments. On the LIBERO benchmark, EMS achieves success rates comparable to the large-scale baseline while increasing the effective action frequency to 93.4 Hz. The framework further demonstrates strong extensibility in real-world dual-arm manipulation tasks, where it accelerates task completion while maintaining robust performance.

</details>


### [75] [LyEvO: Lyapunov-Guided Evolutionary Optimization for Safe and Robust Sim-to-Real Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06481)
*Riccardo Curcio,Hongpeng Cao,Marco Caccamo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LyEvO框架，结合约束进化优化、统计模型检验和Lyapunov稳定性分析，实现仿真到现实的控制器安全鲁棒迁移与部署就绪评估，并在Cartpole和3D四旋翼基准上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真训练的安全鲁棒控制器在真实世界部署前需要一个系统性的就绪评估方法，这是sim-to-real迁移中的关键挑战。

Method: LyEvO利用系统动力学先验知识，通过Lyapunov分析计算初始候选稳定区域，在迭代循环中从该区域采样运行场景来联合优化和统计验证策略，并根据验证结果逐步扩展稳定区域边界。

Result: 在Cartpole和3D Quadrotor基准上通过大量仿真和有目标的真实世界实验，展示了安全鲁棒的sim-to-real迁移性能。

Conclusion: LyEvO提供了一种实用的部署就绪评估准则，能够有效实现仿真到现实的控制器安全鲁棒迁移。

Abstract: Training controllers that are safe and robust in simulation, and systematically assessing their readiness for real-world deployment, remain key challenges in sim-to-real transfer. To address this, we propose LyEvO, a physics-grounded framework that combines constrained Evolutionary Optimization and Statistical Model Checking (SMC)-based verification with Lyapunov-based stability analysis. Leveraging prior knowledge of the system dynamics, LyEvO uses Lyapunov analysis to compute an initial candidate stability region. An iterative loop then uses operational scenarios drawn from this region to jointly optimize and statistically verify a policy, and subsequently expands the region's boundaries based on the verification outcome. This integrated procedure provides a practical criterion for assessing deployment readiness. We evaluate LyEvO on Cartpole and 3D Quadrotor benchmarks through extensive simulations and targeted real-world experiments, demonstrating safe and robust sim-to-real transfer.

</details>


### [76] [A Disturbance in the Force: Force Actuation on the RAVEN II Surgical Robot with Parallel Motor-Cable Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06488)
*Haonan Peng,Dun-Tin Chiang,Jordan Hendricks,Andrew Lewis,Jared Shing,Haokun Feng,Yun-Hsuan Su,Blake Hannaford*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对手术机器人触觉反馈中长期存在的力估计训练数据获取难题，开发了一套平行电机-缆绳系统。该系统通过在工作空间周围安装六个电机-缆绳单元，向机器人末端执行器施加可控外部力，而不干扰手术机器人的运动。初步实验表明力作动精度误差小于1N。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的力估计方法虽有良好精度，但获得具有代表性的训练数据（机器人在各种外力下运动）十分困难，这成为该领域的关键挑战。

Method: 开发一套平行电机-缆绳系统，在工作空间周围安装六个电机-缆绳单元，通过可控张力的缆绳连接到机器人末端执行器，在不干扰手术机器人运动的前提下提供所需的外部力。系统开发涵盖电机单元硬件、控制软件、传感器驱动和仿真等。

Result: 初步实验表明，该系统力作动精度良好，误差小于1牛顿。

Conclusion: 该平行电机-缆绳系统能够有效为手术机器人提供可控的外部力载荷，有望为基于学习的力估计方法生成高质量训练数据，从而推动手术机器人触觉反馈技术的发展。

Abstract: Difficulty in haptic feedback for surgical robots has been a long-term problem for decades. In recent years, learning-based force estimation from robot states suggests desirable accuracy without the necessity of extra sensors. However, challenges remain in obtaining representative training data in which the robot moves in the workspace under various external forces. In this work, a parallel motor-cable system is developed. With six motor-cable units installed around the robot workspace, cables with controllable tension connected to the robot end-effector can provide the desired external force without interfering with the movement of the surgical robot. The development of the system includes motor-unit hardware, control software, sensor drivers, simulations, and more. Preliminary experiments suggest an accuracy of force actuation with errors less than 1 N.

</details>


### [77] [SyncSBC: Decentralized Swarm Behavior Prediction for Synchronized Autonomous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06587)
*Varun Raveendra,Connor Mattson,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SyncSBC，一种完全去中心化的同步蜂群行为分类系统，结合机器学习改进与分布式共识机制，使机器人蜂群能够仅通过局部感知推断群体级行为，实现高精度分类和低同步延迟，并在真实机器人上展示了异常检测和群体行为协调两种应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人群集通过多个独立、感知受限的代理产生复杂的涌现行为，无需中央控制。然而，很少有研究探索代理如何仅凭局部感知推断群集级行为，而这对于检测故障和行为变化至关重要。

Method: SyncSBC将机器学习改进与分布式共识相结合，在完全去中心化的方式下对集体群行为进行分类并同步群决策。系统利用局部感知信息进行行为分类，并通过分布式共识机制同步蜂群的决策结果。

Result: SyncSBC实现了高分类精度和低同步延迟，适合实际部署。在真实机器人上，能够准确识别机器人行为中的异常，并自主协调群行为的集体变化。

Conclusion: SyncSBC为机器人蜂群提供了一种完全去中心化的群体行为分类与决策同步方案，在真实机器人上验证了异常检测和集体行为协调两种有前景的应用场景，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Robot swarms utilize many independent limited-sensing agents to produce complex emergent behaviors without requiring centralized control. However, little research explores how agents can infer swarm-level behavior from purely local perception, a capability critical for detecting faults and behavior changes. In this paper, we introduce Synchronized Swarm Behavior Classification (SyncSBC), which combines improvements in machine learning and distributed consensus to classify collective swarm behavior and synchronize swarm decision-making in an entirely decentralized manner. We show that SyncSBC achieves high classification accuracy and low synchronization delay, making it suitable for real-world deployment. Finally, we use SyncSBC to demonstrate two promising swarm applications on real robots where we show that swarms utilizing SyncSBC can accurately identify anomalies in robot behavior and autonomously coordinate collective changes in swarm behavior. Videos, code and supplemental experiments are available at https://sites.google.com/view/sync-sbc/home.

</details>


### [78] [Plan-and-Avoid: Real-Time Aircraft Trajectory Coordination in a Multi-Agent Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06648)
*Huseyin Emre Tekaslan,Ella M. Atkins,Natasha Neogi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实时"规划与避让"（PAA）框架，用于协调多智能体空域操作，保护声明的优先轨迹。该框架在优先轨迹单独无法保持安全间隔时，生成考虑飞行器约束的单边咨询指令，修改附近飞机轨迹以维持所有交通的安全间隔。基于华盛顿特区真实ADS-B数据进行900余次强迫着陆场景测试，覆盖575次冲突全部生成可行咨询，端到端最坏响应时间5.7秒，93.5%的咨询满足RTCA DO-365标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂的空域环境中，某些飞机（如因紧急情况、任务关键操作或机动受限）必须遵循特定的优先轨迹飞行。现有方法难以在实时条件下协调周围交通来保护这些优先轨迹，因此需要一种能够同时保证优先轨迹不受干扰且所有飞机保持安全间隔的自主协调框架。

Method: PAA框架包含两个核心组件：(1) Plan组件利用应急着陆规划器生成候选优先轨迹；(2) Avoid组件通过不确定性感知的预测模型识别与优先轨迹存在安全间隔违例风险的附近飞机，并生成受飞行器约束的单边避让咨询指令，修改这些飞机的轨迹以维持全体交通的安全间隔。框架通过ADSB真实交通数据进行大规模仿真测试。

Result: 在华盛顿特区空域超过140小时模拟飞行中，对900余次强迫着陆场景进行测试，框架为全部575次独特冲突生成了可行的协作咨询指令。端到端最坏响应时间为5.7秒（含优先轨迹规划、咨询生成及1秒双向数据链延迟），93.5%的咨询满足RTCA DO-365的35秒探测与避让时间阈值。

Conclusion: PAA框架展示了通过实时自动化咨询生成来保护优先轨迹并维持安全间隔的低延迟协调能力，显著提高了复杂空域中多智能体协同操作的安全性和效率。未来工作将量化咨询引起的延迟及其运行影响。

Abstract: This paper presents a real-time Plan-and-Avoid (PAA framework for coordinating cooperative multi-agent airspace operations around a declared priority trajectory. The priority trajectory represents an aircraft flight plan that must be preserved because of constrained maneuverability, an emergency, a mission-critical task, or assigned operational priority. The framework predicts uncertainty-aware, well-clear separation violations with surrounding traffic and, when the priority plan alone cannot maintain separation, generates vehicle-constrained unilateral advisories that modify nearby aircraft trajectories to maintain well-clear separation for all traffic. The approach is applicable to any declared priority trajectory. This paper demonstrates the Plan component using a contingency landing planner to generate candidate priority trajectories. PAA then identifies nearby aircraft passing too close to this priority trajectory and issues Avoid resolution advisories to these aircraft. The framework is tested using real-world Automatic Dependent Surveillance-Broadcast (ADS-B) traffic from the Washington, D.C., airspace across more than 900 forced-landing cases, totaling over 140 hours of simulated flight. The PAA framework generates feasible cooperative advisories for all 575 unique conflict encounters, with a worst-case end-to-end response time of 5.7 s on a personal computer, including priority trajectory planning, advisory generation, and 1 s two-way datalink delay. In total, 93.5% of generated advisories satisfy the 35 s RTCA DO-365 Detect-and-Avoid temporal threshold. These results demonstrate low-latency coordination for preserving priority trajectories while maintaining well-clear separation through real-time automated advisory generation. Future work will quantify advisory-induced delays and their operational impacts.

</details>


### [79] [SoRoMoX: Fast, Differentiable, and Parallelizable Soft Robot Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06650)
*Maximilian Stölzle,Solange Gribonval,Daniel Feliu-Talegon,Vito Daniele Perfetta,Michele Martini,Chuhan Zhang,Kiwan Wong,Mohammed Tarnini,Anup Teejo Mathew,Federico Renda,Daniela Rus,Cosimo Della Santina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SoRoMoX，一个基于JAX的软体机器人建模框架，将Cosserat杆理论模型实现为可微、GPU并行、JIT编译的控制导向工具，相比现有方法在CPU上快18.1倍，GPU并行吞吐量提升234.6倍，并在系统辨识、残余力学习、计算力矩控制、控制增益优化、安全约束控制和强化学习等多个工作流中展现了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cosserat杆理论的软体机器人模型虽然在理论上已经成熟，但现有实现在可微性、GPU并行性和控制导向工作流方面存在不足，无法支持先进刚体机器人应用中常见的计算范式，限制了这些模型在实际控制中的应用。

Method: SoRoMoX是一个完全数值化的Python/JAX框架，采用JIT编译，实现了铰接式（Articulated）、分段常应变（Piecewise Constant Strain）和变应变（Variable Strain）三种模型，通过统一接口提供惯性矩阵、引力与弹性力、雅可比矩阵及其导数，支持端到端微分并可在GPU上直接运行。

Result: CPU顺序仿真比最先进替代方案快18.1倍，GPU并行吞吐量提升234.6倍；系统辨识标记点RMSE降低66%，残差力学习进一步降低64%；计算力矩控制的RMSE相比无模型PD降低约500倍；控制增益优化损失降低62%；安全约束控制将峰值接触力限制在5N内（对比无约束时的33.5N）；强化学习训练速度比CPU基线快7倍。

Conclusion: SoRoMoX填补了Cosserat杆模型在可微、GPU并行和控制导向实现上的空白，证明了高性能数值框架能够解锁以前不可行或不切实际的软体机器人控制工作流，为软体机器人的高级控制方法提供了基础工具。

Abstract: Reduced-order models based on Cosserat-rod theory are now well established, and modeling theory is no longer the primary bottleneck in soft-robot control. Their implementations, however, do not support the differentiable, GPU-parallel, and control-oriented workflows that underpin advanced rigid-robotics applications. Here, we fill this gap with SoRoMoX (Soft Robot Models in JAX), a fully numerical, JIT-compilable Python/JAX framework. SoRoMoX implements articulated, Piecewise Constant Strain, and Variable Strain models through a unified, control-ready interface that provides inertia matrices, gravitational and elastic forces, Jacobians, and their derivatives. To our knowledge, it is the first rod/strain-based soft-robot modeling framework that runs directly on GPUs and is end-to-end differentiable with respect to states, inputs, and parameters. Sequential CPU rollouts are up to 18.1x faster than state-of-the-art alternatives, while GPU-parallel rollouts increase throughput by up to 234.6x. This performance enables workflows that were previously impractical or impossible: static-equilibrium system identification with 66% lower marker RMSE; residual-force learning with a further 64% reduction; computed-torque tracking with RMSE reduced by a factor of approximately 500 relative to model-free PD; control-gain optimization with up to 62% lower loss than untuned gains; safety-constrained control using high-order control barrier functions to keep the peak contact force within a prescribed 5 N bound, compared with 33.5 N without the safety constraint; and reinforcement-learning policy training up to 7x faster than a CPU PyElastica discrete-rod baseline through massively parallel rollouts.

</details>


### [80] [CrossTracer: Cross-Embodiment Navigation via VLA Model Reasoning and Trace Residuals Adapting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06688)
*Yao Wang,Siyuan Wang,Zhirui Sun,Wenzheng Chi,Liang Lin,Jiankun Wang,Wenjun Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出CrossTracer，一种用于跨本体导航的分层框架，通过自适应轨迹残差将语义鸿沟和机器人移动性约束统一。它在NaviTrace基准上以45.68分超越最强通用基线Gemini-2.5-Pro 10.01分，并在轮式和腿式机器人上验证了实际部署效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型为机器人导航提供了强大的语义先验，但往往忽略了具体本体的移动性约束。一个机器人在语义上可行的路径，对另一个机器人可能在物理上不可行。

Method: CrossTracer采用分层框架：1）VL-Tracer利用预训练VLA模型从第一视角观察和灵活目标规格预测初始导航轨迹；2）CE-Adapter通过预测本体条件残差修正来细化轨迹，融合视觉可穿越性线索、机器人身份和初始轨迹；3）训练阶段使用CE-RRT*将全景分割转换为机器人条件化可穿越性成本图，无需昂贵的人工标注。

Result: 在NaviTrace基准上，CrossTracer达到45.68总分，超越最强通用基线Gemini-2.5-Pro达10.01分（28.1%相对提升）。在轮式和腿式机器人的真实世界部署中，导航成功率和执行效率均有提升。

Conclusion: CrossTracer通过统一的像素空间接口和自适应残差机制，有效弥合了语义推理与物理接地之间的鸿沟，实现了跨本体一致的导航轨迹生成，在基准测试和真实部署中均验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide strong semantic priors for robot navigation, but they often ignore embodiment-specific mobility constraints. A path that is semantically plausible for one robot may be physically infeasible for another. We propose CrossTracer, a hierarchical framework for cross-embodiment navigation through adaptive trace residuals. CrossTracer represents navigation plans as normalized image-plane waypoints, forming a unified pixel-space interface between semantic reasoning and physical grounding. First, Vision-Language Trace Proposer (VL-Tracer) adapts a pretrained VLA model to predict an initial navigation trace from egocentric observations and flexible goal specifications. Second, CE-Adapter refines this trace by predicting embodiment-conditioned residual corrections from visual traversability cues, robot identity, and the initial trace. To train the refinement module without costly manual annotation, Cross-Embodiment RRT* (CE-RRT*) converts panoptic segmentation into robot-conditioned traversability cost maps and generates cost-minimizing pixel-space traces. We evaluate CrossTracer on the NaviTrace benchmark, which tests whether a model can generate embodiment-consistent navigation traces from egocentric observations, language instructions, and robot embodiment types. CrossTracer achieves a total score of 45.68, outperforming the strongest evaluated general-purpose baseline, Gemini-2.5-Pro, by 10.01 points, corresponding to a 28.1% relative improvement. Real-world deployment on wheeled and legged robots further shows improved navigation success and execution efficiency.

</details>


### [81] [Hoverflie: An empirical investigation of rotor shrouds to transform micro air vehicles into multi-modal hovercraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06707)
*Mrinmoy Modak,Daniel S. Drew*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对室内/近建筑环境下小型旋翼无人机续航时间极短的问题，设计并实验表征了一种定制护罩系统，将Crazyflie 2.1微型飞行器转变为可在高效气垫船模式与自由飞行无人机模式间切换的多模态机器人。通过参数化测试导管、进气口和喷嘴几何形状，并建立了能捕捉中间高度吸力效应的经验模型，优化后的护罩配置使地面效应力提升近三倍，地面效应下单次充电飞行时间增加60%，并成功演示了空中飞行、近地悬停及模式切换控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型旋翼飞行器（如微型无人机）在室内或建筑周围环境中续航时间极为有限，需要提升其飞行效率和可用时间。

Method: 设计了定制护罩系统将Crazyflie 2.1改造成多模态机器人（气垫船/无人机混合体）；搭建了可精确控制悬停高度、转子占空比和自动记录升力的实验平台；对导管、进气口和喷嘴几何形状进行参数化测试；建立了捕捉中间高度吸力效应的经验模型；采用薄膜热成型工艺制造轻量化护罩；使用简单模式切换控制器实现空中的受控飞行、近地悬停及悬停-飞行过渡。

Result: 优化配置的地面效应力提升近三倍，同时保持相当的离地效应气动推力；地面效应下单次充电飞行时间增加60%，自由飞行仅下降30%（因护罩增加的重量降低控制裕度）；成功演示了多模态受控飞行，并报告了跟踪误差以量化性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为轻量化地面效应飞行器和混合无人机-气垫船系统的研究提供了实验验证且易于采用的基础。

Abstract: Small rotorcraft intended for use indoors or around the built environment have extremely limited flight duration. This paper presents the design and experimental characterization of a custom shroud system that transforms a Crazyflie 2.1 micro air vehicle into a multi-modal robot capable of operating as a high-efficiency hovercraft or a free-flying drone. A custom experimental platform was developed for precise control of hover height and rotor duty cycle, and automated data logging of lift forces. Parametric testing of duct, intake, and nozzle geometries was performed to investigate the impact of shroud configuration on in-ground-effect and free-flight performance. An empirical model is developed which, unlike typical models for ground effect in rotorcraft, captures the suckdown effect that reduces force at intermediate height. It is shown that, through proper design of the shroud, beneficial ground effects can be increased while diminishing negative effects both close to the ground and in free flight. An optimized configuration exhibited nearly three times higher in-ground-effect force while maintaining comparable out-of-ground-effect aerodynamic thrust, although the added shroud mass reduces free-flight control authority. Lightweight shrouds are manufactured using thin-film thermoformed components, and total single-charge flight time is shown to increase by 60% in-ground-effect while decreasing by only 30% in free-flight as compared to the stock drone. Finally, controlled flight in the air, hovering close to the ground, and hover-to-flight transitions are demonstrated using a simple mode-switching controller, with tracking errors reported to quantify performance. This work provides an experimentally-validated and easily adoptable foundation for future research into lightweight ground-effect vehicles and hybrid drone-hovercraft systems.

</details>


### [82] [AtlasVLA: Persistent World-Ego State Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06729)
*Guiyu Zhao,Longteng Guo,Yanghong Mei,Zilin Zhu,Yu Zhang,Bin Cao,Mingming Yu,Xingjian He,Jie Jiang,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AtlasVLA框架，通过双记忆架构（4D持久世界状态记忆和Ego工作状态记忆）将VLA模型从被动反应式操控升级为主动推理式操控，仅凭单目腕部摄像头即可在LIBERO、RLBench和真实世界基准上取得最先进性能，分别超越多视角基线9.4%和17.5%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型本质上是反应式范式，在部分可观测和长时程任务中性能受限。当仅使用单目腕部相机时，会面临物体离开视野导致的感知遗忘以及多步执行过程中的时态任务进度遗忘这两大瓶颈。

Method: AtlasVLA采用双记忆架构：（1）4D持久世界状态记忆，将瞬时的2D观测提升为全局更新的体素哈希空间状态，解决视觉盲区问题；（2）Ego工作状态记忆，跟踪历史自我状态和任务进度。将扩散变换器（DiT）条件化于联合World-Ego状态上，实现鲁棒的空间推理。

Result: 在LIBERO、RLBench和真实世界基准的综合评估中，AtlasVLA仅使用腕部摄像头即达到最先进性能，大幅超越多视角基线，在LIBERO-Long上取得9.4%的绝对成功率提升，在真实世界长时程任务中取得17.5%的提升。

Conclusion: AtlasVLA验证了从被动反应式操控转向主动推理式操控的有效性，通过持久世界-自我状态的双记忆架构，成功解决了单目视觉条件下的感知遗忘和任务进度遗忘问题，为VLA模型在部分可观测长时程场景中的应用提供了新的方向。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have advanced embodied AI, their fundamentally reactive paradigm severely limits performance in partially observable and long-horizon tasks. When restricted to a single wrist-mounted camera, they inevitably suffer from perception forgetting as objects exit the field of view, and temporal task-progress forgetting} during multi-step execution. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose AtlasVLA, a novel framework that transitions from direct reactive manipulation to proactive reasoning through a persistent world-ego state. AtlasVLA features a dual-memory architecture: a 4D Persistent World State Memory that lifts transient 2D observations into a globally updated, voxel-hashed spatial state to resolve visual blind spots, and an Ego-Working State Memory that tracks historical ego state and task progress. By conditioning a diffusion transformer (DiT) on this joint World-Ego state, AtlasVLA enables robust spatial reasoning. Extensive evaluations across LIBERO, RLBench, and real-world benchmarks demonstrate that AtlasVLA achieves state-of-the-art performance using solely a wrist camera. Remarkably, it decisively outperforms multi-view baselines, yielding absolute success rate improvements of 9.4% on LIBERO-Long and 17.5% in real-world long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [83] [Is Forward Prediction Enough? Physical State Grounding for JEPA World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06799)
*Haodong Yan,Jiaguan Zhu,Mingyuan Jia,Ruiqing Yin,Junjie He,Zhide Zhong,Junfeng Li,Jinxuan Lu,Hengtao Li,Tianran Zhang,Jiayi Chen,Wenxuan Song,Wen Chen,Yuxiang Gao,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PSG-JEPA，一种物理接地JEPA世界模型，通过在潜在空间中施加两种互补的接地目标（个体潜在表示接地于机器人本体感知状态，潜在对接地于多时域关节角变化），在不改变推理架构和计算成本的前提下，显著提升潜在表示的可识别性、目标条件规划性能和策略学习效果，在模拟和真实机器人上均超越现有最先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA世界模型虽能学习动作条件预测潜在动力学，但其前向预测目标未显式保证机器人物理状态从单个潜在表示中的可靠识别性，也未保证状态变化可从潜在对中可靠推断，这限制了后续规划与策略的性能。

Method: 提出PSG-JEPA，在标准前向预测目标之外引入两个补充接地目标：(1) 将单个潜在表示接地于机器人本体感知（proprioceptive）状态；(2) 将潜在对接地于多时域关节角变化。两个目标仅在训练期间施加，推理架构和计算成本保持不变。

Result: 在三个层次上进行全面评估：潜在可识别性探测、冻结潜在空间上的目标条件规划、以及模拟和真实机器人上的策略学习。实验表明PSG-JEPA在所有三个层次上均一致优于最先进的潜在世界模型基线。

Conclusion: 通过显式物理接地目标塑造潜在空间，可以有效提升世界模型潜在表示的物理可识别性与下游任务性能，且不增加推理成本，验证了物理接地对世界模型学习的价值。

Abstract: Learning structured and control-relevant latent representations remains a key challenge for world models. Recent JEPA-based world models learn action-conditioned predictive latent dynamics from observation sequences. However, their forward-prediction objectives do not explicitly enforce reliable identifiability of robot-centric physical state from individual latents or state changes from latent pairs, which can limit downstream planning and policy performance. We propose PSG-JEPA, a physically grounded JEPA world model that shapes its latent space with two complementary grounding objectives beyond forward prediction: grounding individual latents in robot proprioceptive state, and grounding latent pairs in multi-horizon joint-angle changes. Both objectives are applied only during training, leaving the inference architecture and computational cost unchanged. To comprehensively evaluate PSG-JEPA, we conduct experiments at three levels: (1) latent identifiability via probing, (2) goal-conditioned planning on frozen latents, and (3) policy learning in simulation and on a real robot. Experiments demonstrate that our PSG-JEPA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art latent world-model baselines at all three levels.

</details>


### [84] [R2S-EGO: Dual-Proxy Refinement for Sparse-Capture Real-to-Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06827)
*Shuai Fang,Xin Deng,Yuchen Kang,Zhenjiang Li,Jie Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: R2S-EGO通过模拟器导出的机器人代理定义行为域，结合采集锚定的几何代理提供结构条件，在固定预算下选择性地生成伪观测以补全缺失视角，提升机器人真实到仿真迁移的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实到仿真(R2S)依赖沿机器人ego轨迹渲染观测的场景表示，但密集多视角采集效率低，稀疏人工采集可能导致机器人行为范围内的视角支持不足。

Method: 提出R2S-EGO框架：(1)耦合模拟器导出的机器人代理表示行为可执行的查询域；(2)结合采集锚定的几何代理提供场景结构条件；(3)在固定预算下基于当前支持缺陷选择目标视角生成观测；(4)生成观测作为伪观测细化视觉资产，真实采集保持锚点；(5)融合几何代理提供碰撞表面并在多轮间刷新。

Result: 在48个冻结Unitree G1 ego视角、三个Replica场景中，六视角R2S-EGO达到19.062 dB PSNR，远超最强R2S基线14.226 dB。在五个配对的策略训练种子中，R2S-EGO在真实G1坐立任务中达到82.5%±6.8%成功率，而GaussGym仅为10.0%±10.5%。

Conclusion: R2S-EGO通过行为域引导的相机控制合成和采集锚定的几何条件，有效解决了稀疏采集中机器人视图支持不足的问题，显著提升了R2S的观测质量和下游策略性能，同时保持机器人动态和控制栈不变。

Abstract: Real-to-sim (R2S) depends on scene representations that render observations along robot ego trajectories, yet dense multi-view capture limits per-environment real-image capture-count efficiency, and sparse human capture can
  leave behavior-scoped robot views under-supported. Camera-controlled synthesis can fill missing views, but its use in R2S requires behavior-admissible queries and capture-anchored structural conditioning. We present R2S-EGO,
  which couples a simulator-derived robot proxy that represents the behavior-scoped executable query domain with a capture-anchored geometry proxy that supplies scene-specific structural conditions. Within this domain, fixed-
  budget selection targets current support deficits for which geometry support is available. The generated observations are assimilated as pseudo-observations to refine the visual asset, while real captures remain anchors. The
  fused geometry proxy also supplies the scene collision surface, which is refreshed between rounds. Together, these updates refine the existing simulation scene while its robot dynamics and control stack stay fixed. Across 48
  frozen Unitree G1 ego views in three Replica scenes, six-view R2S-EGO reaches 19.062 dB PSNR, compared with 14.226 dB for the strongest reported R2S baseline. Across five paired policy-training seeds, R2S-EGO achieves 82.5%
  +/- 6.8% real-G1 sitting success, compared with 10.0% +/- 10.5% for GaussGym.

</details>


### [85] [When Coordination Becomes a Threat: Communication Attacks in LLM-Controlled Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06830)
*Zhen Huang,Zhihuang Liu,Weijia Shi,Yifan Yang,Weishang Wu,Zhiping Cai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM规划器在具身多机器人系统中的通信安全风险，提出两种通信攻击（外部入口攻击和特权系统内攻击），在三种通信架构（DMAS、HMAS-1、HMAS-2）下评估其影响，并引入CPV门控机制来缓解风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用作具身多机器人系统中的高层规划器，但其安全风险未被充分研究。已有工作主要关注单个机器人的安全问题，对多机器人协作中的通信风险理解不足，且现有多机器人研究仅在DMAS架构下进行了初步分析，不清楚风险是否在其他常见通信架构中持续存在以及攻击者访问设置如何影响风险传播。

Method: 作者提出了两种通信攻击：外部入口点攻击（External Entry Point Attack）和特权系统内攻击（Privileged In-System Attack），对应不同的攻击者访问设置。在三种架构（DMAS、HMAS-1、HMAS-2）上使用三个LLM和五个具身多机器人任务进行评估。此外，提出了CPV（Claim Provenance and Verification）门控机制，对通信中的声明在下游复用前进行来源验证。

Result: 不安全信息在三种架构下均可转化为不安全行为：DMAS达到96.7%的入口认可率和100%的认可后激活率，HMAS-1达到97.8%的不安全动作成功率，HMAS-2触发88.3%的任务定义不安全动作槽。CPV门控将违规率从70.0%降至36.6%。

Conclusion: 多机器人协作中的通信风险在所有三种常见架构下均普遍存在，CPV门控机制通过在信息流下游使用前进行声明验证，能有效降低安全违规率，为多机器人LLM规划器的安全性提供了防御思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used as high-level planners in embodied multi-robot systems, enabling robots to interpret natural language instructions and coordinate executable actions. Yet, this growing reliance on LLM planners also raises security concerns. Prior work has focused mainly on individual robots, while communication risks in multi-robot collaboration remain insufficiently understood. Existing multi-robot studies are further limited to preliminary analysis under the Decentralized Multi-agent System (DMAS) architecture, so it remains unclear whether these risks persist across other common communication architectures and how attacker access settings shape their propagation. To fill this gap, we formulate two communication attacks corresponding to distinct attacker access settings: the External Entry Point Attack and the Privileged In-System Attack. We evaluate both attacks across DMAS, HMAS-1, and HMAS-2 using three LLMs and five embodied multi-robot tasks. Results show that unsafe information can turn into unsafe actions across all three architectures: DMAS reaches a 96.7\% entry endorsement rate and a 100\% post endorsement activation rate, HMAS-1 reaches a 97.8\% unsafe action success rate, and HMAS-2 triggers 88.3\% of task defined unsafe action slots. To mitigate risks from trusted information flow, we introduce the Claim Provenance and Verification (CPV) Gate, which verifies communicated claims before downstream reuse and reduces the violation rate from 70.0\% to 36.6\%.

</details>


### [86] [Unordered Landmark Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06833)
*Hao Ren,Junzhe Zhu,Yihan Li,Zetong Bi,Le Zheng,Zhi Li,Yiqing Yuan,Zhaoliang Wan,Dizhe Zhang,Lu Qi,Hui Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ULVN框架，一种无需时序和里程计先验的RGB-only图像目标导航方法，通过构建2D拓扑地图与基于图置信传播的定位规划，解决无序图像集合下的感知混叠和映射失败问题，在仿真和真实环境中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像目标导航方法过度依赖时序视频流或辅助传感器（深度、LiDAR）来维持空间一致性，这严重限制了在众包或预录制的无序图像集合场景下的可扩展性。当去除时序先验后，现有方法面临严重的感知混叠、噪声关联和灾难性映射失败问题。

Method: ULVN是一个统一的RGB-only框架，通过三个关键机制解决无序图像导航：(1)利用校准几何验证和最大生成森林细化从非结构化图像构建鲁棒的2D拓扑地图；(2)摒弃顺序启发式，采用基于图的置信传播滤波器配合熵自适应融合实现全局定位；(3)动态子目标规划用于闭环执行。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界部署中的大量实验表明，ULVN在图像目标导航性能上显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: ULVN通过消除对时序和里程计先验的依赖，成功拓展了图像目标导航在无序图像集合场景下的适用性，并通过系统地整合建图、定位与规划来缓解误差累积问题，为无时序先验的导航提供了一种可行且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Image-goal navigation is a fundamental capability for embodied AI, yet its practical deployment is strained by strong prior assumptions. Existing methods predominantly rely on temporally ordered video streams or auxiliary sensors (e.g., depth, LiDAR) to maintain spatial consistency. These sequential and multimodal dependencies severely restrict scalability, especially when deploying robots using crowd-sourced or pre-recorded unordered image collections. When temporal priors are removed, current methods struggle with severe perceptual aliasing, noisy associations, and catastrophic mapping failures. To address this underexplored challenge, we propose Unordered Landmark Visual Navigation (ULVN), a unified RGB-only framework free from temporal and odometric priors. ULVN systematically mitigates error accumulation by integrating mapping, localization, and planning. Specifically, it constructs a robust 2D topological map directly from unstructured images via calibrated geometric verification and maximum spanning forest refinement. For closed-loop execution, ULVN abandons sequential heuristics, utilizing a graph-based belief propagation filter with entropy-adaptive fusion for global localization and dynamic subgoal planning. Extensive experiments in simulation and real-world deployments demonstrate that ULVN significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [87] [Are Visual Place Recognition Models Recognizing Places or Conditions? Distractor-Augmented Evaluation and Condition Suppression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06847)
*Beomsu Kim,Minwoo Jung,Giseop Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对长期视觉地点识别（VPR）中众包地图数据库包含跨条件干扰图像的问题，提出Distractor-Augmented Recall (DAR)指标来量化和评估干扰项的影响，并采用条件抑制方法（INLP和LEACE）去除描述符中的条件信息（如光照、天气、季节），实验表明方法排名在DAR@1下与R@1显著不同，且条件抑制在不降低R@1的情况下普遍提升DAR@1表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的VPR评估通常在单一条件下进行查询与数据库匹配，但众包地图数据库可能混合多种条件，且包含与查询条件相似但属于不同地点的干扰图像。现有的VPR方法可能因条件相似性而非地点身份进行检索，导致判别能力反而让他们编码了光照、天气和季节等信息，从而对干扰项敏感。

Method: 1) 提出Distractor-Augmented Recall (DAR)指标，用于隔离和量化干扰项对VPR性能的影响；2) 提出条件抑制方法，利用INLP和LEACE等线性概念消除技术从VPR描述符中移除条件信息（光照、天气、季节等）；3) 在11种VPR方法和6个数据集上进行系统评估。

Result: 1) 在DAR@1下的方法排名与Recall@1 (R@1)下的排名存在显著差异，说明干扰项鲁棒性与标准检索性能是不同维度的能力；2) 应用INLP和LEACE作为条件抑制方法，普遍提高了DAR@1表现，同时不降低R@1性能。

Conclusion: VPR方法对干扰项的鲁棒性与标准检索性能是独立的能力维度，通过抑制描述符中的条件信息可以在不牺牲标准检索性能的前提下有效提升干扰项鲁棒性，为长期VPR系统的实际部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Long-term Visual Place Recognition (VPR) is typically evaluated by matching queries from one condition against a database from another. Crowdsourced map databases, however, may mix conditions and include images that resemble the query in condition but depict different places. In the presence of these distractors, a method may retrieve by condition similarity rather than place identity. We argue that this susceptibility arises because the discriminability of VPR methods allows them to encode information such as illumination, weather, and seasonal appearance in their descriptors. We therefore introduce Distractor-Augmented Recall (DAR) to isolate and quantify the effect of distractors, and propose condition suppression to remove condition information from VPR descriptors. Across eleven methods and six datasets, method rankings under DAR@1 differ from those under Recall@1 (R@1), while applying INLP and LEACE as condition suppression methods generally improves DAR@1 without reducing R@1. Thus, distractor robustness is distinct from standard retrieval performance and can be improved by suppressing condition information.

</details>


### [88] [How Should I Pick a Foundation Model for My Robot? In Favor of a Community Evaluation Framework for Social Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06898)
*Eric Nichols,Alva Markelius,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对社交机器人基础模型选型难题，提出了一个包含五个评估维度（对话能力、用户安全、具身角色、目标场景有效性、受众适配性）的三层漏斗式评估范式，从通用指标逐步过渡到模拟交互，最终进行代价高昂的机器人专属评估，并呼吁社区共同构建评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者构建基于基础模型的社交机器人应用时面临模型选型困难：公共排行榜缺乏实时、具身社会交互场景的指导，而直接评估在规模化层面不现实（需要稀缺的参与者、机器人和实验者资源），迫切需要低成本且信息充分的评估方案。

Method: 提出三层漏斗式评估范式：第一层用通用指标进行筛选过滤，第二层扩展到模拟交互，第三层进行更昂贵的机器人专属评估。同时将五个评估维度映射到三个层级上，梳理各维度上已有和缺失的评估方法。

Result: 明确了五个评估维度在三个评估层级上的完整映射，指出了当前评估方法在各维度各层级上的覆盖情况与空白，为社交机器人基础模型的系统化评估提供了框架性指导。

Conclusion: 当前缺少面向社交机器人基础模型的系统化评估框架，本文提出的三维度-三层级评估范式可作为起点，并号召研究社区共同协作构建完整的评估体系。

Abstract: Researchers who seek to build social robot applications on foundation models are faced with a difficult question: how should we pick a model? Public leaderboards offer little guidance: the demands of real-time, embodied social interaction lie largely outside their focus. And direct evaluation is impractical at scale: each embodied study requires scarce participant, robot, and experimenter time. In this paper, we identify five evaluation dimensions for foundation models in social robots: (i) conversational competence, (ii) user safety, (iii) embodied character, (iv) target scene effectiveness, and (v) audience appropriateness. To make model selection cheaper and better informed, we propose a three-tiered evaluation funnel paradigm that first filters with general metrics, then extends to simulated interactions, and terminates in more expensive, robot-specific evaluation. We map all five dimensions across all three tiers, chart where applicable evaluation methods exist and are missing, and close with a call to action: let's build the evaluation framework together as a community.

</details>


### [89] [Spatiotemporal Agility: Time-Constrained Reinforcement Learning for Vision-Guided Dynamic Quadrupedal Interception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06907)
*Yidong Zhu,Zibo Dai,Tongning Zhang,Leixin Chang,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向腿足机器人的动态球捕捉集成框架，将视觉模块（预测落点和时间）与直接以位置和时间条件化的强化学习运动策略相结合，替代传统的速度跟踪策略，并构建了从多摄像头感知到仿真到现实部署的完整闭环系统，实验证明该方法在动态拦截任务中具有更高的成功率和更好的sim-to-real迁移表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有四足运动方法大多基于速度跟踪策略，难以在严格时间约束下精确到达目标位置；同时，将实时感知与敏捷运动集成到动态目标上仍面临传感器延迟和处理延迟的挑战，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 提出一个集成框架：(1) 视觉模块预测球的落点和到达时间；(2) 直接以目标位置和时间作为条件的RL运动学策略（而非中间速度指令）；(3) 构建完整的实时拦截系统，包括多摄像头感知、在线轨迹预测、低延迟目标通信和sim-to-real运动控制，形成闭环部署管线。

Result: 与速度跟踪基线相比，直接目标条件化方法在预测落点2米内、飞行时间0.8-1.2秒的球捕捉任务中取得了更高的成功率，且部署后性能差距更小，表明sim-to-real迁移行为更好，机器人成功完成了动态球捕捉任务。

Conclusion: 通过显式预测未来的时空目标，该方法有效缓解了动态拦截中的感知延迟问题，验证了直接目标条件化策略相比传统速度跟踪策略在敏捷动态任务中的优势，为腿足机器人实现高动态精准交互提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Legged robots require robust agility to perceive and interact with complex and dynamic environments within a constrained time. However, most existing quadruped locomotion works rely on velocity-tracking policy, which struggle to reach precise targets within strict temporal constraints. Moreover, integrating real-time perception with agile locomotion for highly dynamic targets remains challenging due to sensor latency and processing delays. To concretely study and benchmark such agility in dynamic settings, we introduce a challenging ball-catching task for legged robots. This paper proposes an integrated framework that combines a vision module for landing point and time prediction with a direct position and time conditioned RL locomotion policy, instead of intermediate velocity commands. Beyond the method design, this work presents a system-level contribution that completes real-time robotic interception system that integrates multi-camera perception, online trajectory prediction, low-latency target communication, and sim-to-real locomotion control into a closed-loop deployment pipeline. By explicitly predicting the future spatial-temporal target, our approach mitigates perception latency during dynamic interception. We conducted extensive ball-catching experiments for the legged robot. Through comparative experiments against a velocity-tracking baseline, our direct target-conditioned approach achieves a higher success rate in catching balls with predicted landing spots within 2 meters and flight times between 0.8 and 1.2 seconds. This shows that the robot has successfully completed the dynamic ball-catching task under our tested setup. Furthermore, our policy exhibits a smaller performance gap after deployment, suggesting improved sim-to-real behavior in these trials.

</details>


### [90] [Cross-View Action Consistency for Camera-Robust Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06965)
*Bingqi Huang,Bingchuan Wei,Xuan Wang,Yingkai Cai,Zhaokui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究VLA策略在场景相机移动时的鲁棒性问题，提出通过动作等价视角对和流匹配正则化来提升场景相机视角鲁棒性，在LIBERO基准上达到87.2%的准确率，真实机器人成功率达74.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略从固定场景相机微调后，当相机位置改变时性能会显著下降，即使任务、物体、语言和机器人状态均未改变。现有方法依赖相机标签、外参、深度或点云输入，本文希望在无需这些辅助信息的前提下仅依靠场景RGB图像、语言和本体感觉来提升视角鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 屏蔽手腕流（wrist stream），防止未受扰动的视觉捷径干扰场景相机变化的影响归因；2) 对基于流的VLA模型，对动作流速度场进行正则化——将原始LIBERO演示重置到相同MuJoCo状态，渲染标称和扰动的场景相机视图构建动作等价视角对；3) 两视图均由流匹配监督，跨视图损失强制其在相同采样流坐标下预测的动作流速度一致。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus相机扰动测试集上达到87.2±0.4%（3个训练种子各4,797次回滚），相比仅流匹配训练提升+7.4pp，相比朴素混合相机SFT提升+12.5pp，同时保持名义相机ID性能（95.0±0.8%）。打乱配对的控制实验降至25.8%，证明增益依赖动作等价配对。真实机器人三个桌面任务中，未见相机位姿成功率从53.3%提升至74.4%。

Conclusion: 动作等价视角对配对训练结合跨视角流速度一致性正则化，能够在不依赖相机标签、深度或点云等额外信息的情况下显著提升VLA策略的场景相机视角鲁棒性，且不损失名义相机性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies fine-tuned from a fixed scene camera can fail when the camera is moved, even when the task, objects, language, and robot state are unchanged. We study scene-camera viewpoint robustness using only a scene RGB image, language, and proprioception, without camera labels, extrinsics, depth, or point-cloud inputs. The wrist stream is masked throughout to prevent an unperturbed visual shortcut from confounding attribution to scene-camera variation. For flow-based VLAs, we propose to regularize the action-flow velocity field, the quantity directly integrated to generate continuous action chunks. We construct action-equivalent view pairs by resetting original LIBERO demonstrations to the same MuJoCo state and rendering nominal and perturbed scene-camera views. Both views are supervised by flow matching, while a cross-view loss encourages their predicted action-flow velocities to agree at the same sampled flow coordinates. On the LIBERO-Plus camera-perturbation track, our method reaches 87.2$\pm$0.4% (4,797 rollouts per seed across 3 training seeds), +7.4pp over flow-matching-only training on the same paired data (79.8$\pm$0.8%, also 3 seeds) and +12.5pp over naive mixed-camera SFT, while maintaining nominal-camera ID performance (95.0$\pm$0.8%; same-data FM-only: 95.0$\pm$4.3%). A shuffled-pair control collapses to 25.8%, showing that the gain depends on action-equivalent pairing. On a real robot, we evaluate three tabletop tasks with 10 rollouts per task and camera placement; held-out-camera success improves from 53.3% to 74.4% under the same single-scene-RGB inference interface.

</details>


### [91] [Exact Thrust-Reversal Limits of Bidirectional Propellers under Bounded Motor Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06991)
*Ahmed Ali,Chiara Gabellieri,Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了双向螺旋桨推力反转的固有限制：由于推力是转速的符号二次函数，推力过零时输入增益消失，有界电机力矩无法实现通用推力反转。作者推导了归一化推力坐标模型，证明了可行性条件取决于期望推力的过零阶数，并通过实验验证了不同反转阶数对推力跟踪的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向螺旋桨常被视为有符号推力源，但实际上其推力是转速的符号二次函数，推力反转必然经过零转速点，此时有界电机力矩改变推力的能力崩溃。这一作动器层面的根本限制尚未被系统形式化，对飞行机器人的力、加速度和交互控制参考设计有重要影响。

Method: 建立了推力坐标系下的归一化模型，其中推力为零时输入增益消失；从数学上证明了有界电机输入下精确推力轨迹可复现的充要条件与期望推力的过零阶数相关；推导了直流电机驱动双向螺旋桨时电流和电压的正则性要求；并通过电机-螺旋桨实验验证了理论预测。

Result: 证明通用推力反转（推力以非零斜率过零）需要无界电机输入；给出了设计推力反转以避免奇异电机指令的设计准则；实验表明线性反转会产生局部电流/电压尖峰和推力跟踪性能下降，而高阶反转则不会。

Conclusion: 揭示了双向螺旋桨作动器层面的固有局限，该限制在飞行机器人的力、加速度及交互控制参考设计中必须被考虑。研究为设计更平滑、物理可实现的推力反转指令提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Bidirectional propellers are often treated as signed thrust sources, but their thrust is a signed-quadratic function of rotor speed.Thus, thrust reversal necessarily occurs through zero rotor speed, where the ability of a bounded motor torque to change thrust collapses.This work formalizes this obstruction by studying exact thrust-trajectory reproducibility under bounded motor inputs with prescribed smoothness.We derive a normalized thrust-coordinate model with vanishing input gain at zero thrust, and prove necessary and sufficient reproducibility conditions in terms of the zero-crossing order of the desired thrust.Generic reversals, in which thrust crosses zero with nonzero slope, require unbounded motor input; the resulting conditions provide direct design rules for shaping thrust reversals that avoid singular motor commands.We also derive the corresponding current and voltage regularity requirements for a DC motor driving a bidirectional propeller.Experiments on a motor-propeller setup validate the predicted reversal-order effects, showing localized current/voltage peaks and thrust-tracking degradation for linear reversals, but not for higher-order reversals.These results expose an intrinsic actuator-level limitation that must be considered in force, acceleration, and interaction-control references for aerial robots.

</details>


### [92] [Decoupling Intention from Trajectory: A Representational Deduction Framework for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06994)
*Xiangkai Ma,Yue Ma,Junjie Wang,Sheng Xu,Mingyang Li,Han Zhang,Yuzheng Zhuang,Wenzhong Li,Zhihao Yuan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PILOT框架，通过核心的表示演绎（RD）方法将运动思维链（CoT）引导作为世界动作模型（WAM）的原生能力，显式建模潜在状态转换token，从而解耦高层物理状态演化与低层动作轨迹生成，提升复杂机器人操作任务的成功率、泛化能力和物理可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型的视觉分支只预测静态视觉观测，未能反映运动交互下世界状态演化的潜在转换信息，导致高层物理条件演化与低层动作轨迹生成之间的表征纠缠，形成结构瓶颈并削弱世界演化建模对动作生成的预测能力。

Method: 提出PILOT框架，其核心是表示演绎（RD）方法：该方法将运动思维链（CoT）引导整合为模型原生能力，促使动作分支显式建模潜在状态转换token，并在推理空间中保留为CoT以引导细粒度运动轨迹生成。

Result: 实验表明RD显著提升了WAM在复杂机器人操作任务中的成功率和泛化能力，增强了模型的物理可解释性（通过解耦高层运动语义与低层轨迹细节），同时丰富的状态转换监督信号缓解了动作生成的稀疏监督问题，可作为高效的小样本真实机器人微调策略，并具有良好的跨主流WAM架构迁移扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过引入潜在状态转换的显式建模与CoT引导，PILOT有效解决了WAM中表征纠缠的结构瓶颈，在提升任务性能的同时增强了物理可解释性和数据效率，为世界动作模型的发展提供了新的方向。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) aim to construct a unified architecture capable of understanding world state evolution and guiding to generative motion planning. However, existing visual branches focus on predicting static visual observation, rather than reflecting potential transition information that captures the evolution of world states under motion interactions. This leads to representational entanglement between high-level physical condition evolution and low-level action trajectory generation within the Action Model, creating a structural bottleneck while weakening the predictive capability of world evolution modeling for action generation. We propose PILOT (Physical Inference for Latent Optimized Trajectories), whose core Representational Deduction (RD) bridges this gap by integrating motion thought-of-chain (CoT) guidance as a native model capability. Specifically, RD aims to encourage the action branch to explicitly model potential state transition tokens, which are retained as CoT in the reasoning space to guide fine-grained motion trajectory. Experiments demonstrate that RD not only significantly improves the success rate and generalization ability of WAMs in complex robotic manipulation tasks but also enhances the model's physical interpretability by decoupling high-level motion semantics from low-level trajectory details. Furthermore, the abundant state transition supervision signals introduced by RD effectively alleviate the sparse supervision in action generation, enabling it to serve as an efficient few-shot real-robot fine-tuning strategy and demonstrating superior scalability for migration to mainstream WAM architectures.

</details>


### [93] [Automated Terminal-to-Housing Assembly System for Flat Ribbon Cable Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06996)
*Eunkyu Choi,Joonho Seo,Seungmin Lee,Seokhwan Jeong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向双向单排扁平排线（FRCH）的传感器最小化自动装配系统，通过纯机械序列（线缆对齐、倾斜滑动、编织、夹紧）实现多端子耦合插入，在80次试验中达到83.75%的端到端成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统装配研究多聚焦于销孔插入或单端子插入任务，而FRCH装配涉及柔性、密集几何约束下的机械耦合多端子插入，现有自动化方案难以应对该复杂场景。

Method: 提出纯机械装配序列：Cable Alignment、Lean & Slide、Weaving和Clamping四个步骤，逐步减小端子间的相关性错位、插入干涉和不稳定性，不依赖主动传感或视觉。

Result: 80次试验中端到端成功率为83.75%（前半段85.0%，后半段82.5%），半速运行下循环时间为33秒。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了面向多针脚壳体FRCH端子到壳体装配的自动化原型系统，为柔性多端子装配提供了新的机械式解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a sensor-minimal automated assembly system for bidirectional single-row flat ribbon cable harnesses (FRCHs). Unlike conventional peg-in-hole or single-terminal insertion tasks, FRCH assembly involves mechanically coupled multi-terminal insertion under flexible and dense geometric constraints. To address this problem, the proposed system performs the assembly through a purely mechanical sequence consisting of Cable Alignment, Lean & Slide, Weaving, and Clamping, without relying on active sensing or vision. Each mechanism is designed to progressively reduce correlated terminal misalignment, insertion interference, and instability before final locking. Experiments on bidirectional single-row FRCHs achieved an 83.75% end-to-end process success rate over 80 trials, with success rates of 85.0% and 82.5% in the first and second halves, respectively. The cycle time was 33 s under half-speed operation. To the best of our knowledge, this work presents the first automated prototype for FRCH terminal-to-housing assembly for multi-pin housings.

</details>


### [94] [A Haptic Robot Finger Designed for Guqin Instrument Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07002)
*Tianwei Zhang,Hanming Yan,Yang Yang. Ziya Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种高精度触觉感知手指的设计与验证，通过模仿人类指尖和指甲形态开发仿生多模态触觉指尖，并在古琴弦接触任务（包括空弦与按音比较、泛音调弦和触觉触发的双手协调）中进行了验证，旨在解决现有演奏机器人仅依赖开环位置控制、无法胜任需要灵巧手和精密触觉感知的乐器操作问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐演奏机器人主要采用开环位置控制，无法操作需要灵巧手和精确触觉感知的乐器（如小提琴、吉他、古琴等），因此需要开发具备高精度触觉感知能力的指尖来弥补这一技术空白。

Method: 通过仿生学方法，模拟人类手指的指尖和指甲形态，设计并开发了一种仿生多模态触觉指尖（高精度触觉感知手指），并以古琴为验证场景，在空弦与按音比较、泛音调弦、触觉触发的双手协调等弦接触任务上进行验证。

Result: 该触觉感知手指在选定的古琴弦接触任务上成功完成验证，包括空弦与按音比较、泛音调弦以及触觉触发的双手协调等任务，证明了仿生多模态触觉指尖的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究将触觉感知与机器人技术相结合，为需要灵巧操作和精密触觉感知的乐器演奏机器人提供了技术基础，同时在世界文化遗产保护和中华文化传播方面具有应用价值。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of humanoid robotics and embodied intelligence technologies, numerous musical instrument-playing robots have emerged in recent years, such as pianos, chime bells, and taiko drums. These robots primarily employ open-loop positional control, rendering them incapable of operating instruments requiring dexterous hands and precise tactile perception, such as a violin, guitar, and guqin. This paper describes the design and validation of a high-precision tactile-sensing finger. By mimicking the shape of the fingertip and fingernail found on a human finger, we develop a biomimetic multimodal haptic fingertip and validate it on selected guqin string-contact tasks, including open-string and stopped-note comparisons, harmonic-tuning, and tactile-triggered bimanual coordination, using the guqin, a traditional Chinese musical instrument, as a challenging validation scenario rather than as a fully demonstrated robotic performance system. This research integrates tactile sensing with robotics technology, thereby contributing to applications in world heritage conservation and cultural dissemination.

</details>


### [95] [Benchmarking and Reasoning Distillation of Large Language Models for Feedback Controller Design in Complex Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07004)
*Zhongchao Zhou,Yixuan Xie,Wenwei Yu,Yuxi Lu,Yaonan Zhu,Qian Niu,Yutaka Matsuo,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了CoDyControlBench基准，包含132个系统配置、5个评估维度，用于评测LLM在复杂反馈控制器设计任务上的表现。评测了6个主流LLM，GPT表现最佳（94.8%成功率），并开发了1.5B参数的推理蒸馏模型，在物理实验中成功实现了气动人工肌肉机器人手臂的轨迹跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在控制器设计领域的评测主要局限于线性单自由度系统和大型API模型，缺乏对复杂控制器设计任务和边缘部署可行性的评估，因此需要更全面的基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了涵盖132个系统配置的CoDyControlBench基准，围绕5个维度（自由度、系统类型、耦合度、阻尼区间、控制器类型）评估6种LLM；并通过推理蒸馏技术开发了1.5B参数的轻量级边缘部署模型。

Result: GPT以94.8%的设计成功率位居第一，Qwen最低为50.0%；自由度数和控制器类型对成功率影响最大。推理蒸馏模型在基准上优于答案蒸馏模型和基座模型，并成功在3次物理试验中完成气动人工肌肉机器人手臂的目标跟踪。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了控制器设计任务的LLM评测基线，证明了知识蒸馏方法在保留控制设计能力的同时实现边缘部署的可行性，展示了轻量级模型在实际物理系统上的应用潜力。

Abstract: Although remarkable capabilities have been demonstrated by Large Language Models (LLMs) across scientific domains, feedback controller design remains underexplored. Existing benchmarks focus mainly on linear single-Degree-of-Freedom (DoF) systems and large API-hosted models, leaving performance on complex controller-design tasks and feasibility for edge deployment unclear. To address these limitations, we introduce the Complex Dynamics-to-Control Benchmark for Large Language Models (CoDyControlBench), comprising 132 system configurations across five evaluation dimensions: number of DoF, system type, coupling level, damping regime, and controller type. Six state-of-the-art LLMs were evaluated over three independent runs, including three commercial models (GPT, Gemini, and Claude) and three open-source models (GLM, DeepSeek, and Qwen). GPT achieved the highest design success rate at 94.8\%, whereas Qwen showed the lowest rate at 50.0\%. Across the benchmark dimensions, DoF and controller type exhibited the largest model-averaged variations in design success, with success-rate ranges of 36.3\% and 17.6\%, respectively, exceeding those associated with system type, coupling level, and damping regime. Comparison of GPT and Qwen showed that their performance gap arose mainly from the control-design knowledge, particularly gain selection and the use of transient-limiting mechanisms. For edge deployment, a specialized 1.5B-parameter model was developed through reasoning distillation. The reasoning-distilled model outperformed the answer-distilled and base model on CoDyControlBench, maintained stable performance across 1-6 DoFs, and achieved successful traget tracking in all three physical trials on a pneumatic-artificial-muscle-driven robotic arm. These results establish a benchmark baseline and highlight the potential of lightweight, edge-deployable controller-design models.

</details>


### [96] [Real-time Whole-Body Motion Planning for Mobile Manipulators Carrying Arbitrarily Shaped Payloads via Kinematically-Coupled SVSDF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07005)
*Yisheng Li,Longji Yin,Tingrui Zhang,Ruize Xue,Haoda Zhu,Nan Chen,Siqi Liang,Yuxi Liu,Fu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向移动操作臂搬运任意形状大尺寸非凸载荷的实时全身运动规划框架。前端采用链式分解的核函数碰撞检测保留真实几何信息；中端将路径转化为光滑可行轨迹并直接执行；后端基于KC-SVSDF进行轨迹优化，通过运动链传播避碰梯度。实验结果验证了该框架在狭窄通道和杂乱环境中可靠搬运非凸载荷的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作臂在杂乱环境中搬运大型非凸载荷时，现有规划器要么过于简化载荷几何，要么无法处理机械臂连杆间的运动学耦合，导致可行空间丢失或优化停滞，亟需一种能同时兼顾真实几何和运动学耦合的规划框架。

Method: 提出三阶段规划框架：前端采用链式分解的核函数碰撞检测（保留机器人及载荷真实几何，支持紧凑存储和快速位级查询）；中端将前端路径转换为连续光滑且满足动力学可行性的轨迹，并在无碰撞时直接执行以绕过昂贵后端；后端在需要精化时基于运动学耦合的SVSDF（KC-SVSDF）进行轨迹优化，沿运动链传播避碰梯度以生成连贯的全身逃逸方向。

Result: 通过消融实验、与最新基线方法的对比基准测试，以及在差速驱动移动操作臂上的真实世界实验，验证了所提框架能够在狭窄通道和杂乱环境中可靠地搬运大型非凸载荷。

Conclusion: 该框架通过前端真实几何碰撞检测、中端快速直通策略与后端运动学耦合轨迹优化的协同设计，有效解决了移动操作臂搬运大型非凸载荷的实时全身规划难题，兼顾了规划效率与轨迹可行性。

Abstract: Mobile manipulators are increasingly tasked with transporting large, non-convex payloads through cluttered environments, yet existing planners either oversimplify the payload geometry or fail to handle the kinematic coupling between manipulator links, leading to lost feasible space or stalled optimization. This letter presents a real-time whole-body motion planning framework for mobile manipulators carrying arbitrarily shaped payloads. The front-end employs a chain-decomposed kernel-based collision check that preserves the true geometry of the robot and payload, with compact storage and fast bit-level queries. A mid-end preprocessing stage converts the front-end path into a continuous trajectory enforcing smoothness and feasibility, and executes it directly when collision-free to bypass the costly back-end. When refinement is required, the back-end performs trajectory optimization built on a Kinematically-Coupled SVSDF (KC-SVSDF), which propagates collision-avoidance gradients along the kinematic chain to produce coherent whole-body escape directions. Ablation studies, comparative benchmarks against state-of-the-art baselines, and real-world experiments on a differential-drive mobile manipulator demonstrate that the proposed framework reliably transports large, non-convex payloads through tight passages and cluttered environments.

</details>


### [97] [C2Dex: Contact-Consistent Reconstruction and Retargeting for Dexterous Manipulation from Monocular Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07045)
*Jie Ren,Zhehao Jiang,Yinhong Yang,Haorui Jia,Han Jiang,Ben Li,Yao Yao,Cheng Lin,Qiu Shen,Zhenshan Bing,Xiao-Xiao Long,Xun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出C2Dex框架，通过共享交互表征（稳定的物体侧接触）将单目人类视频转化为灵巧机器人操作演示，在DexYCB和TACO上分别达到57.78%和26.67%的轨迹成功率，远超基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧机器人操作的高质量演示数据昂贵且难以收集，而单目人类视频虽然易获取，但现有方法在重建手-物交互时存在时间不稳定、物理不可行的问题，且重定向方法难以在不同手部形态间保持任务相关接触和局部交互几何。

Method: C2Dex围绕共享交互表征构建，通过在规范物体空间中聚合噪声逐帧观测来恢复稳定的物体侧接触。这些稳定接触发挥双重作用：作为轨迹级约束引导重建出时间连贯且物理合理的HOI轨迹；作为灵巧手的显式迁移目标，通过拉普拉斯交互优化保持跨形态的局部手物几何，并通过残差强化学习在仿真中细化轨迹。

Result: 在DexYCB和TACO数据集上，C2Dex分别实现57.78%和26.67%的端到端轨迹成功率，显著优于最强基线（17.78%和10.00%）；真实机器人重放实验证明了在多种接触密集型操作任务中的物理可行性。

Conclusion: C2Dex通过稳定的共享交互表征成功弥合了单目人类视频与灵巧机器人操作之间的差距，验证了从视频到灵巧操作学习路径的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: High-quality demonstrations for dexterous robot manipulation are costly and difficult to collect, whereas monocular human videos provide a scalable source of diverse manipulation behaviors. However, transferring such demonstrations to dexterous robots remains challenging: monocular hand-object interaction (HOI) reconstruction often produces temporally unstable contacts and physically implausible interactions, while conventional retargeting methods struggle to preserve task-relevant contacts and local interaction geometry across different hand embodiments. We present C2Dex, a video-to-dexterous-manipulation framework built around a shared interaction representation: stable object-side contacts recovered by aggregating noisy frame-wise observations in the canonical object space. These stable contacts serve a dual role: as trajectory-level constraints that guide reconstruction toward temporally coherent and physically plausible human HOI trajectories, and as explicit transfer targets for the dexterous hand, where Laplacian interaction optimization preserves the local hand-object geometry across embodiments and residual reinforcement learning refines the trajectory in simulation. Experiments on DexYCB and TACO show that C2Dex achieves end-to-end trajectory success rates of 57.78% and 26.67%, respectively, substantially outperforming the strongest baselines (17.78% and 10.00%) under identical evaluation criteria. Real-robot replay experiments further demonstrate physical feasibility across diverse contact-rich manipulation tasks. Project page: https://k-jie.github.io/C2Dex/

</details>


### [98] [AutoIntervene: Calibrated Intervention for Action-Chunking Imitation Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07065)
*Jinhe Tang,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoIntervene是一个在线框架，在执行过程中根据视觉-动作支持记忆对动作块策略与操作员之间进行选择性控制切换，支持阶段局部和全局支持，提高任务成功率和降低操作员控制时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作块视觉运动策略虽然通过预测短动作序列提高了时间一致性，但在感知错误和执行漂移下可能离开演示分布，产生与当前状态不一致的平滑但错误的动作块，缺乏有效的在线干预机制。

Method: 构建视觉-动作支持记忆库，结合视觉相似度和动作一致性评估候选动作块；采用阶段局部支持（策略→操作员切换）和全局支持（操作员→策略恢复）的双向切换机制；从专家演示的评估得分经验分位数自动校准切换阈值；保留干预片段用于后续策略更新。

Result: 在真实双臂操作任务中，该方法相比手动干预，在适应后任务成功率更高、操作员控制时间更短。

Conclusion: AutoIntervene通过基于支持记忆的选择性控制转移和自动阈值校准，有效改善了动作块策略在部署时的可靠性与学习适应性。

Abstract: Action-chunking visuomotor policies learn from demonstrations and improve temporal consistency by predicting short action sequences rather than single-step commands. Yet perception errors and execution drift can move the robot outside the demonstration distribution, while the policy continues to produce smooth action chunks that are inconsistent with the observed state. We present AutoIntervene, an online framework that selectively transfers control between an action-chunking policy and an operator during deployment. AutoIntervene evaluates proposed chunks against a visual-action support memory built from successful task executions, combining visual similarity with consistency between proposed and reference actions. Phase-local support governs policy-to-operator transfer within the current task phase, whereas global support governs the return to policy control after operator recovery. We calibrate separate switching thresholds for the two directions from empirical quantiles of evaluation-level scores on held-out expert demonstrations, avoiding direct manual tuning of score cutoffs. Intervention segments retained from successful rollouts target learner-induced states and provide corrective supervision for subsequent policy updates. Experiments on real-world bimanual manipulation tasks show higher post-adaptation task success and lower operator-control time than manual intervention. Videos and additional results are available at https://aus.bot/research/autointervene/.

</details>


### [99] [M2-SMap: Memory-Efficient Semantic Mapping with Hierarchical Multi-Model Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07074)
*QiYing Deng,ZhongLai Wang,Yuan Gao,Wei Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出M2-SMap，一种基于分层多模型表示的内存高效语义建图框架，通过分层几何分解、投影引导的语义标注和对象感知的高斯融合策略，在RGB-D序列上实现实时运行（≥29.37 Hz）并显著降低原始数量（平均减少18.7%），同时将帧间对象粘连案例从2.808降至0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集点云地图内存消耗随场景规模快速增长，难以部署于资源受限的机器人；紧凑单模型表示几何表达能力不足，而现有基于局部几何的多模型方法容易导致过拟合和对象间粘连。

Method: 1) 分层几何分解将RGB-D点云划分为紧凑的高斯组件；2) 投影引导的语义标注机制为每个组件分配实例身份；3) 将标注融入对象感知的高斯融合策略；4) 多尺度特征提取分离大面积平面区域、语义对象和复杂残差结构，分别用有界平面、对象级超二次曲面和高斯混合模型（GMM）表示。

Result: 在三个RGB-D序列上，M2-SMap以不低于29.37 Hz的帧率实时运行，达到最低的原始数量，较最优基线平均减少18.7%；同时将平均每帧测得的对象间粘连案例从2.808降至0。

Conclusion: M2-SMap通过分层多模型表示和语义感知融合，实现了高效且语义一致的场景表示，有效解决了对象间粘连问题并显著降低内存开销。

Abstract: Dense point cloud maps, as a typically used mapping representation, are difficult to deploy on resource-constrained robots because their memory consumption grows rapidly with scene scale. Although compact single-model representations reduce memory cost, their fixed geometric expressiveness is insufficient for structurally diverse environments. Existing multi-model methods improve representational flexibility, yet their feature extraction and model selection are often dominated by local geometry, which can cause overfitting and adhesion between objects. To address these issues, this paper presents M2-SMap, a memory-efficient semantic mapping framework based on hierarchical multi-model representation. First, a hierarchical geometric decomposition partitions RGB-D point clouds into compact Gaussian components. Then, a projection-guided semantic annotation mechanism assigns instance identities to each component. Subsequently, these annotations are incorporated into an object-aware Gaussian fusion strategy. Furthermore, a multi-scale feature extraction strategy separates large planar regions, semantic objects, and complex residual structures, which are respectively represented by bounded planes, object-level superquadrics, and GMM primitives. Experiments on three RGB-D sequences show that M2-SMap runs in real time at no less than 29.37 Hz while achieving the lowest primitive count, with an average reduction of 18.7% over the best baseline. It also reduces the mean per-frame number of measured inter-object adhesion cases from 2.808 to 0, demonstrating efficient and semantically consistent scene representation.

</details>


### [100] [Detection and Ranging of Transient Extrinsic Contacts Based on 6D Dynamic Tactile Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07075)
*Haowen Zheng,Yinghao Wu,Fuyuan Liu,Yichen Li,Yitian Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TECDAR方法，利用一个2.5×3 mm的6D惯性测量单元以7 kHz采样率进行动态触觉传感，通过扩展卡尔曼滤波融合触觉数据与机器人位姿，实现对被抓取物体与环境之间瞬时外部接触的快速检测与定位，定位精度约7 mm，响应时间180 ms，并能在毫秒级尺度上纠正机器人轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精细操作常涉及被抓物体与环境之间瞬时、微妙的碰撞，人手凭借优异触觉灵敏度可轻松定位这些接触，但机器人系统缺乏足够分辨率的传感能力来获取运动规划所需信息，导致操作笨拙或任务失败。

Method: 提出TECDAR系统：在夹爪尖端设计动态触觉传感方案，采用单个2.5×3 mm的6D惯性测量单元（IMU），以7 kHz采样率捕获亚毫秒级指尖变形，数据流仅84 KB/s。通过扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）融合触觉数据与机器人位姿，实现对外部接触的快速精确定位。

Result: 系统在线接触和点接触定位任务中平均定位精度约为7 mm，可在180 ms内达到毫米级精度；近瞬时定位能力使机器人能在毫秒级尺度上纠正轨迹，支持精确工具操作和复杂环境感知。

Conclusion: TECDAR通过高带宽、紧凑数据的动态触觉传感，显著提升了机器人在精细操作中的外部接触检测与定位能力，有望在精密装配、手术辅助及触觉主导环境的自主探索等领域推动机器人技术进步。

Abstract: Delicate manipulation often involves transient and subtle collisions between a grasped object and the environment. While the human hand localizes these contacts effortlessly thanks to superior tactile sensitivity, robotic systems often lack the requisite resolution to acquire the information necessary for motion planning, resulting in clumsy manipulation or even task failure. Here, we propose transient extrinsic contact detection and ranging (TECDAR), a simple yet fast and efficient method for detecting and ranging extrinsic contact of grasped objects. Our design of gripper tips employs dynamic tactile sensing leveraging a single 2.5$\times$3 mm 6D inertial measurement unit. The sensor captures sub-millisecond tip deformations at a 7 kHz sampling rate, but operating on a data stream of only 84 KB/s. High bandwidth and compact data size enable the system to rapidly detect and localize contact between grasped objects and their surroundings. Specifically, fusing tactile data with robot pose via an extended Kalman filter enables fast and precise localization of extrinsic contact, reaching millimeter-level accuracy within 180 ms. Experimental results demonstrate that the system achieves an average localization accuracy of approximately 7\,mm in both line-contact and point-contact localization tasks. Furthermore, this near-instantaneous localization enables the robot to rectify its trajectory on a millisecond scale, facilitating precise tool manipulation and enhanced perception of complex environments purely through tactile exploration and mapping. We envision such techniques advancing the future of robotics across domains requiring delicate manipulation, including precision assembly, surgical assistance, and autonomous exploration in touch-dominant environments. Project page: humitlab.github.io/TECDAR/

</details>


### [101] [LifelongCrossNav: Persistent 3D Semantic Memory for Cross-Floor Multi-Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07079)
*Zehui Li,Zihao Sun,Jiawei Xu,Zheqi He,Xiaoqiang Zhang,Jing-Shu Zheng,Lu Liu,Dahui Gao,Xiuwan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了LifelongCrossNav框架，用于未知多楼层室内环境中的顺序多目标导航，通过共享稀疏3D语义体素记忆和跨楼层可穿越性建模，结合新基准HM3D-MFMON验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的目标导航工作虽然在语义感知和探索方面取得了进展，但多目标导航的持久记忆和跨楼层导航通常被分开研究，缺乏统一的解决方案。

Method: 构建共享稀疏3D语义体素记忆，增量累积几何结构、可穿越状态和视觉-语言特征；结合支持感知的3D可穿越性映射、楼梯特定感知和方向感知的楼梯遍历；统一导航策略协调同层前沿探索、实时/历史兴趣点检索、楼梯导航和目标搜索等子任务；并创建HM3D-MFMON基准。

Result: 在HM3D-MFMON基准上，LifelongCrossNav持续优于代表性的平面持久语义地图基线方法，证明了持久3D语义记忆和跨楼层可穿越性建模对顺序多目标导航的有效性。

Conclusion: 持久3D语义记忆与跨楼层可穿越性建模能够有效支持多楼层环境中的顺序多目标导航任务。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation has made substantial progress in semantic perception and exploration, yet persistent memory for multi-object navigation and cross-floor navigation are still commonly addressed separately. We present LifelongCrossNav, a framework for sequential multi-object ObjectNav in unknown multi-floor indoor environments. Within each episode, the agent receives an ordered sequence of object-goal queries while continuously maintaining a shared sparse 3D semantic voxel memory. This memory incrementally accumulates geometric structure, traversability states, and vision-language features, allowing subsequent object-goal queries to retrieve previously acquired scene information without rebuilding the map. To support persistent search across floors, LifelongCrossNav combines support-aware 3D traversability mapping, stair-specific perception, and direction-aware stair traversal. A unified navigation policy coordinates same-floor frontier exploration, live and historical point-of-interest retrieval, stair navigation, and target-object search and approach. We further introduce HM3D-MFMON, a benchmark for sequential Multi-Floor Multi-Object Navigation built on HM3D scenes, including a dedicated subset in which completing the full sequence of object-goal subtasks requires at least one floor transition. Experimental results show that LifelongCrossNav consistently outperforms a representative planar persistent semantic-map baseline on HM3D-MFMON, demonstrating that persistent 3D semantic memory and cross-floor traversability modeling effectively support sequential multi-object navigation in multi-floor environments. Project page: https://flageval-baai.github.io/LifelongCrossNavPage.

</details>


### [102] [Identifying the Key Biomechanical Features of Movement Adaptation during Exoskeleton-Assisted Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07140)
*Peter Seungjune Lee,Katja Mombaur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过9名健康受试者在三种条件下（无外骨骼、外骨骼主动踝关节助力、外骨骼零力矩）的 treadmill 步行实验，分析下肢运动学、关节间协调性和代谢成本的时间演化，发现外骨骼辅助下的适应过程是渐进的、高度个体化的，且运动学适应在下肢各关节间异步发生，代谢反应具有异质性且常不收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对外骨骼辅助行走过程中人类自然适应的理解有限，尤其是个体差异和适应的时间进程缺乏深入研究，而现有文献普遍采用稳态假设，可能无法准确捕捉适应的动态特性。

Method: 9名健康受试者在三种条件下进行 treadmill 步行：无外骨骼、外骨骼主动踝关节助力、外骨骼零力矩。在群体和个体层面分析下肢运动学、关节间协调性以及代谢运输成本（MCoT）。

Result: 适应是渐进且高度个体化的，参与者间的收敛时间和运动模式存在显著变异性。运动学适应在下肢各关节间异步发生，摆动阶段波动较大。代谢反应具有异质性且常常不收敛，揭示出现有文献中常用的稳态假设的局限性。

Conclusion: 研究强调了个体层面时间演化分析对理解外骨骼使用中适应动态的重要性，表明需要超越群体平均和稳态假设来分析适应过程。

Abstract: The understanding of natural human adaptation during exoskeleton-assisted locomotion - particularly individual differences in adaptation behaviors and temporal progression - remains limited. In this work, we investigate temporal evolution of biomechanical variables to uncover participant-specific adaptation strategies across different exoskeleton-assisted locomotion scenarios. Nine healthy participants performed treadmill walking under three conditions: without an exoskeleton, with exoskeleton active ankle assistance, and with exoskeleton zero-torque. Lower limb kinematics, inter-joint coordination, and metabolic cost of transport (MCoT) were analyzed at both the group and individual levels. Results indicate that adaptation is gradual and highly individualized, with substantial variability in convergence timing and movement patterns across participants. Kinematic adaptation occurred asynchronously across lower limb, with larger fluctuations during the swing phase. Metabolic responses were heterogeneous and often non-convergent, highlighting the limitations of steady-state assumptions commonly adopted in the literature. These findings emphasize the importance of individual-level, temporal evolution analyses for understanding adaptation dynamics in exoskeleton use.

</details>


### [103] [Representation Handoffs for OpenArm-Based Laboratory Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07154)
*Yang Shen,Chonghao Cheng,Ziyi Zhao,Jialuo Zhu,Zhenyi Yi,Qi Zhao,Jian Yang,Yuhui Shi,Chin-Teng Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于OpenArm的移动操作机器人原型，用于实验室自动化任务。系统通过"表示交接"架构将自然语言指令约束为技能调用，将传感器观测落地为地图和物体位姿，并利用运行时绑定编译可执行的运动目标，通过干运行追踪和启动检查来暴露部署阻断问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源机器人和基础模型降低了具身AI的门槛，但语言引导的实验室自动化仍需要从指令和观测到安全动作的可靠对齐，目前缺乏有效的中间表示来调试语言、感知、规划与机器人安全之间的集成。

Method: 构建OpenArm双机械臂移动操作原型，集成移动底座、垂直滑轨、RGB-D感知、激光雷达建图、ROS2/MoveIt执行和配置文件定义的技能接口。系统围绕表示交接组织，包括将自然语言请求约束为已注册技能调用、传感器观测落地为地图和目标位姿、物体先验提供角色与技能约束、运行时绑定将已验证技能编译为可执行运动目标。

Result: 通过干运行追踪和启动检查评估集成路径，显示原型将缺失校准、不完整物体资产和未完成的真实场景视觉落地暴露为明确的部署阻断因素。

Conclusion: 中间表示可作为具身系统中集成语言、感知、规划和机器人安全的实用调试接口，帮助识别和定位部署阶段的关键问题。

Abstract: Open-source robotics and foundation models have lowered the barrier to embodied AI, yet language-guided laboratory automation still requires reliable alignment from instructions and observations to safe actions. This field report presents an OpenArm-based mobile manipulation prototype for laboratory-style tasks, built by integrating dual OpenArm manipulators with a mobile base, vertical slide, RGB-D sensing, lidar-based mapping, ROS2/MoveIt execution, and profile-defined skill interfaces. The system is organized around representation handoffs: natural language requests are constrained into registered skill calls, sensor observations are grounded into maps and object poses, object priors provide role and skill constraints, and runtime bindings compile validated skills into executable motion goals. We use dry-run traces and startup checks to evaluate this integration path, showing how the prototype exposes missing calibration, incomplete object assets, and unfinished real-scene visual grounding as explicit deployment blockers. These intermediate representations serve as practical debugging interfaces for integrating language, perception, planning, and robot safety in embodied systems.

</details>


### [104] [TEMPO: Semantic-Action Decoupled RL Post-Training for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07314)
*Ziheng Liu,Quantao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TEMPO是一个面向VLA模型的语义-动作解耦双时间尺度RL后训练框架，通过冻结视觉语言骨干网络，分别以不同频率更新语义投影层和低层动作专家，在CALVIN基准和真实操作任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型微调方法存在不足：SFT容易产生分布不匹配问题，而现有RL后训练方法对所有模型组件采用统一的更新策略，忽视了不同组件在模型中的功能差异。

Method: TEMPO框架包含三个关键设计：(1) 冻结预训练的视觉语言骨干网络以保留通用语义表征；(2) 将适应范围限制为语义投影层和低层动作专家两个组件，各自使用独立的RL优化循环；(3) 采用双时间尺度更新策略：语义投影层低频更新以保持潜在动作稳定，动作专家高频更新以快速从在线交互中吸收控制反馈。

Result: 在CALVIN基准和真实世界操作任务上的实验表明，TEMPO一致性地超越了预训练SOTA VLA模型和RL后训练基线，并在两个真实世界任务上达到并保持了更高的评估奖励。

Conclusion: 解耦的RL微调策略能够防止快速策略更新破坏高层语义表征，同时仍允许动作专家高效地从在线反馈中学习，为VLA模型的RL后训练提供了一种更精细化的组件级优化方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are commonly adapted to downstream manipulation tasks via supervised fine-tuning (SFT) or online reinforcement learning (RL) post-training. SFT is prone to distribution mismatch, and existing RL approaches typically apply a single, uniform update strategy to all model components, ignoring their distinct functional roles. We propose TEMPO, a semantic-action decoupled, two-timescale RL post-training framework for VLA models. TEMPO freezes the pretrained vision-language backbone to preserve general semantic representations, and restricts adaptation to two components with dedicated RL optimization loops: the semantic projection layer and the low-level action expert. We update them at different rates--the semantic projection layer infrequently, to keep the latent action stable, and the action expert frequently, to rapidly incorporate control feedback from online interaction. This decoupling RL fine-tuning strategy prevents fast policy updates from destabilizing high-level semantic representations while still allowing the action expert to learn efficiently from online feedback. Experiments on the CALVIN benchmark and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that TEMPO consistently outperforms both pretrained state-of-the-art VLA models and the RL post-training baseline, while reaching and maintaining higher evaluation rewards on two real-world tasks.

</details>


### [105] [Learning Fault-Tolerant Locomotion with Adaptive Gait Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07328)
*Giovanbattista Gravina,Luca Rossini,Carlo Rizzardo,Arturo Laurenzi,Nikos Tsagarakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度强化学习的容错运动控制方法，用于四足机器人在执行器功率损失情况下维持稳定和机动性。方法采用非对称Actor-Critic架构，引入潜在对齐损失和学习步态频率参数，在仿真和68kg真实四足机器人上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件故障（如执行器功率损失）要求腿式机器人快速重组协调和步态时序以维持稳定，但对于大型四足机器人，由于质量增加和执行器驱动限制更紧，在小平台上有效的激进高频补偿策略难以适用，因此需要新的容错控制方法。

Method: 采用深度强化学习框架：1) 非对称Actor-Critic架构，训练时Critic可获取特权信息，Actor通过本体感觉观测重构对应的潜在表征；2) 引入潜在对齐损失，促进Actor与Critic表征之间的一致性；3) 在动作空间中增加可学习的步态频率参数，实现自适应步态节奏，无需预定义故障腿策略。

Result: 该方法在不平坦地形的高保真仿真以及68kg四足机器人的平地真实实验中均得到验证，证明在部分执行器功率损失情况下仍能保持稳定和机动性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法通过将特权信息蒸馏到Actor的潜在表征中，并学习自适应步态频率，能够有效应对执行器功率损失，且在大质量四足机器人场景下展现良好的容错运动能力。

Abstract: Hardware failures require legged robots to rapidly reorganize coordination and gait timing to maintain stability and mobility. This is particularly challenging for larger quadrupeds, where increased mass and tighter actuation limits reduce the feasibility of aggressive, high-frequency compensation strategies often observed on smaller platforms. In this work, we propose a deep reinforcement learning approach for fault-tolerant locomotion under actuator power loss. The method employs an asymmetric actor-critic architecture in which the critic has access to privileged information during training, while the actor learns to reconstruct a corresponding latent representation from proprioceptive observations. We introduce a latent-alignment loss that encourages consistency between actor and critic representations. Additionally, we augment the action space with a learnable gait frequency parameter, enabling adaptive gait timing in response to terrain variations and actuator degradation without predefined faulty-leg strategies. The approach is validated in high-fidelity simulation on uneven terrain and real-world experiments on flat ground using a 68 kg quadruped robot.

</details>


### [106] [Depth-Wise Probing and Pruning of the Planning Token in a Driving Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07361)
*Harisankar Babu,Benjamin Coors,Christopher Lang,Hendrik Berkemeyer,Tamim Asfour,Simon Foell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过将规划器作为轨迹空间logit透镜，对VLA模型的32个解码器层逐一解码规划token，发现语义意图在第一层即可线性解码（97.7%准确率），而与原生规划器的兼容性则需到最后一层才达到最佳（Avg-L2 2.11m）。研究发现规划信息其实早已存在，只是格式不兼容，且可移除8/32层解码器，获得约5%误差增加和1.33倍速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将驾驶决策路由通过深层语言模型，但动作本身是否真的需要如此深度的模型并不清晰。作者希望诊断各解码器层的信息分布，探究是否存在冗余深度可被剪枝。

Method: 借用生成式规划器作为轨迹空间logit透镜，从32个解码器层中的每一层解码规划token，测量两组信号：(1)导航命令的线性可解码性（命令探针准确率）；(2)与冻结原生规划器的轨迹兼容性（开放循环Avg-L2）。同时通过排名各层对规划token的角度偏差来指导层移除。

Result: 命令探针在第一层后即达97.7%准确率（随机基线16.7%），表明语义意图早期可线性解码；而兼容性随深度逐渐提升，Avg-L2仅在最后一层达到最小值2.11m。第一层的可学习读出可以恢复大部分差距，证实规划信息早期已存在但格式不兼容。可移除8/32层，误差增加仅约5%，实测解码器加速1.33倍。

Conclusion: VLA模型中语义意图在浅层即可获得，而深层主要负责将已有信息重新格式化为部署规划器可消费的形式。解码器深度存在可观的冗余，可被剪枝以提升效率而不引入显著性能损失。结果限于ORION检查点和Bench2Drive设置。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models route driving decisions through a deep language model, but it is unclear how much of that depth the action itself requires. We study a representative driving VLA whose entire plan is carried by a single planning token that a generative planner decodes into a trajectory. Borrowing the planner as a trajectory-space logit lens, we decode the planning token from every one of the 32 decoder layers and measure two signals: the linear decodability of the navigation command and trajectory compatibility with the frozen native planner. Our diagnostic shows that semantic intent is linearly decodable early: command-probe accuracy reaches 97.7\% after the first decoder layer, compared with 16.7\% chance. In contrast, compatibility with the frozen native planner improves gradually across depth, with open-loop Avg-L2 reaching its minimum of 2.11\,m only at the final layer. Learned readouts from the first layer recover much of this gap, indicating that planning information is already present early but is not yet represented in the format expected by the deployed planner. Ranking decoder layers by the angular deviation they induce in the planning token permits removal of 8 of 32 layers within an approximately 5\% relative open-loop error increase and yields a measured 1.33$\times$ decoder speedup. At the evaluated sample size, no family-specific degradation is statistically resolved. These findings are limited to the evaluated ORION checkpoint and Bench2Drive setup.

</details>
